# Art prompts

Generated by `yarn art:catalog` from the cards (754 images). Do not edit it by hand.

## ui

**card-back.png** (1024×1280)

Collectible card game card back design, painted cartoon style, very vivid and saturated colors, high contrast, clean thick outlines, perfectly symmetric ornamental composition centered on the card, vertical framing. In the center a glowing golden hourglass with violet sand inside a circular clockwork emblem of interlocking brass gears and a clock dial with roman numerals, radiant light rays behind it. Deep violet and indigo background with a subtle starry time vortex and faint swirling arcane patterns. Ornate gold filigree border with small hourglass and gear motifs in the four corners. No text, no letters, no logo, no watermark
